```
- path: src/app/actions/checkout.ts
  content: |
      'use server';
      
      import { z } from 'zod';
      import { getCustomerSession, touchSession } from '@/services/customer-session';
      import { validatePromoCode } from '@/services/promo';
      import { createOrder } from '@/services/order';
      import { checkoutSchema } from '@/lib/validations';
      
      export type PromoPreviewResult =
        | { ok: true; code: string; name: string; discount: number }
        | { ok: false; error: string };
      
      export async function validatePromoAction(
        code: string,
        subtotal: number
      ): Promise<PromoPreviewResult> {
        const session = await getCustomerSession();
        if (!session) return { ok: false, error: 'Sesi tidak ditemukan.' };
      
        if (!Number.isFinite(subtotal) || subtotal <= 0) {
          return { ok: false, error: 'Subtotal tidak valid.' };
        }
        return validatePromoCode(code, subtotal);
      }
      
      export type CheckoutResult =
        | { ok: true; orderToken: string }
        | { ok: false; error: string };
      
      /**
       * Buat order dari keranjang customer.
       * Seluruh validasi (session, produk, stok, promo, harga, status toko)
       * dilakukan di server — data dari browser tidak dipercaya.
       */
      /** Data pemesan tersimpan untuk prefill checkout berikutnya. */
      export async function getCustomerProfileAction(): Promise<{
        ok: boolean;
        name: string;
        phone: string;
      }> {
        const session = await getCustomerSession();
        if (!session) return { ok: false, name: '', phone: '' };
        const { db } = await import('@/db');
        const { customers } = await import('@/db/schema');
        const { eq } = await import('drizzle-orm');
        const [row] = await db
          .select({ name: customers.name, phone: customers.phone })
          .from(customers)
          .where(eq(customers.id, session.customerId))
          .limit(1);
        return { ok: true, name: row?.name ?? '', phone: row?.phone ?? '' };
      }
      
      export async function createOrderAction(rawInput: unknown): Promise<CheckoutResult> {
        // 1. Session wajib valid
        const session = await getCustomerSession();
        if (!session) {
          return {
            ok: false,
            error: 'Sesi kamu sudah berakhir. Silakan masukkan token kembali.',
          };
        }
      
        // 2. Validasi bentuk input
        const parsed = checkoutSchema.safeParse(rawInput);
        if (!parsed.success) {
          const firstError = parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? 'Data tidak valid.';
          return { ok: false, error: firstError };
        }
        const input = parsed.data;
      
        // 3. Toko COD harus aktif (dicek dari DB)
        const { getStoreInfo } = await import('@/services/store');
        const store = await getStoreInfo();
        if (input.paymentMethod === 'cod' && store && !store.codEnabled) {
          return { ok: false, error: 'Pembayaran COD sedang tidak tersedia.' };
        }
      
        // 4. Buat order (semua aturan bisnis di dalam service)
        const result = await createOrder({
          sessionId: session.sessionId,
          customerId: session.customerId,
          name: input.name,
          phone: input.phone,
          note: input.note || null,
          paymentMethod: input.paymentMethod,
          promoCode: input.promoCode || null,
          items: input.items,
        });
      
        if (!result.ok) {
          return { ok: false, error: result.error };
        }
      
        await touchSession(session.sessionId);
        return { ok: true, orderToken: result.orderToken };
      }
      
      // ============================================================
      // QRIS: BUKTI TRANSFER & KONFIRMASI "SUDAH BAYAR"
      // ============================================================
      
      /**
       * Upload bukti transfer + tandai menunggu verifikasi.
       * Order wajib milik session pemanggil (Rule 14).
       */
      export async function submitPaymentProofAction(
        orderToken: string,
        formData: FormData
      ): Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string }> {
        try {
          const session = await getCustomerSession();
          if (!session) return { ok: false, error: 'Sesi berakhir. Masukkan token kembali.' };
      
          // 1. Order harus milik session ini
          const { db } = await import('@/db');
          const { orders } = await import('@/db/schema');
          const { and, eq } = await import('drizzle-orm');
          const [order] = await db
            .select({ id: orders.id, paymentMethod: orders.paymentMethod, paymentStatus: orders.paymentStatus })
            .from(orders)
            .where(and(eq(orders.orderToken, orderToken), eq(orders.sessionId, session.sessionId)))
            .limit(1);
          if (!order) return { ok: false, error: 'Pesanan tidak ditemukan.' };
          if (order.paymentMethod !== 'qris') {
            return { ok: false, error: 'Pesanan ini bukan pembayaran QRIS.' };
          }
          if (order.paymentStatus === 'paid') {
            return { ok: false, error: 'Pesanan ini sudah dibayar.' };
          }
      
          // 2. Validasi file sama seperti upload admin (PRD §84)
          const file = formData.get('file');
          if (!(file instanceof File)) return { ok: false, error: 'File tidak ditemukan.' };
          const ALLOWED = ['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'image/webp'];
          if (!ALLOWED.includes(file.type)) return { ok: false, error: 'Format harus JPG/PNG/WebP.' };
          if (file.size > 2 * 1024 * 1024) return { ok: false, error: 'Ukuran maksimal 2MB.' };
      
          const { saveUploadedImage } = await import('@/lib/storage');
          const proofPath = await saveUploadedImage(file, 'proofs');
      
          // 3. Simpan path bukti + status menunggu verifikasi (sekali transaction)
          const { payments } = await import('@/db/schema');
          await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
            await tx
              .update(payments)
              .set({ proofPath, status: 'waiting_verification' })
              .where(eq(payments.orderId, order.id));
            await tx
              .update(orders)
              .set({ paymentStatus: 'waiting_verification' })
              .where(eq(orders.id, order.id));
          });
      
          // 4. Realtime → admin langsung tahu ada bukti baru
          const { publishRealtimeEvent } = await import('@/services/realtime');
          publishRealtimeEvent({
            topic: 'admin',
            type: 'order.changed',
            payload: { orderToken, paymentStatus: 'waiting_verification' },
          });
      
          return { ok: true };
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('[submitPaymentProofAction]', err);
          return { ok: false, error: 'Gagal mengirim bukti transfer. Coba lagi.' };
        }
      }
      
      /**
       * Bukti tanpa file (customer menekan "Sudah Bayar" saja):
       * status → waiting_verification, admin cek mutasi manually.
       */
      export async function markQrisPaidAction(
        orderToken: string
      ): Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string }> {
        try {
          const session = await getCustomerSession();
          if (!session) return { ok: false, error: 'Sesi berakhir. Masukkan token kembali.' };
      
          const { db } = await import('@/db');
          const { orders, payments } = await import('@/db/schema');
          const { and, eq } = await import('drizzle-orm');
          const [order] = await db
            .select({ id: orders.id, paymentMethod: orders.paymentMethod, paymentStatus: orders.paymentStatus })
            .from(orders)
            .where(and(eq(orders.orderToken, orderToken), eq(orders.sessionId, session.sessionId)))
            .limit(1);
          if (!order) return { ok: false, error: 'Pesanan tidak ditemukan.' };
          if (order.paymentMethod !== 'qris') {
            return { ok: false, error: 'Pesanan ini bukan pembayaran QRIS.' };
          }
          if (order.paymentStatus === 'paid') {
            return { ok: false, error: 'Pesanan ini sudah dibayar.' };
          }
      
          await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
            await tx
              .update(payments)
              .set({ status: 'waiting_verification' })
              .where(eq(payments.orderId, order.id));
            await tx
              .update(orders)
              .set({ paymentStatus: 'waiting_verification' })
              .where(eq(orders.id, order.id));
          });
      
          const { publishRealtimeEvent } = await import('@/services/realtime');
          publishRealtimeEvent({
            topic: 'admin',
            type: 'order.changed',
            payload: { orderToken, paymentStatus: 'waiting_verification' },
          });
      
          return { ok: true };
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('[markQrisPaidAction]', err);
          return { ok: false, error: 'Gagal mengonfirmasi pembayaran. Coba lagi.' };
        }
      }
      
  referencedBy: {}
```

**🛠 Write File**

```json
{
  "path": "src/app/(customer)/checkout/qris-pay-modal.tsx",
  "instructions": "Popup QRIS saat checkout: QR + download + tombol Sudah Bayar + upload bukti transfer.",
  "content": "'use client';\n\nimport { useState } from 'react';\nimport { CheckCircle2, Download, Upload } from 'lucide-react';\nimport { Button, Modal, useToast } from '@/components/ui';\nimport { markQrisPaidAction, submitPaymentProofAction } from '@/app/actions/checkout';\nimport { formatRupiah } from '@/utils';\n\n/**\n * Popup pembayaran QRIS — muncul saat checkout setelah order dibuat\n * (metode QRIS). Berisi QR, tombol download, konfirmasi \"Sudah Bayar\",\n * dan upload bukti transfer.\n */\nexport function QrisPayModal({\n  open,\n  onClose,\n  orderToken,\n  total,\n  qrisImagePath,\n  qrisReceiverName,\n  onPaid,\n}: {\n  open: boolean;\n  onClose: () => void;\n  orderToken: string;\n  total: number;\n  qrisImagePath: string | null;\n  qrisReceiverName: string | null;\n  /** Dipanggil setelah customer menekan \"Sudah Bayar\" / kirim bukti. */\n  onPaid?: () => void;\n}) {\n  const { push } = useToast();\n  const [confirming, setConfirming] = useState(false);\n  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);\n  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);\n\n  const qrisUrl = qrisImagePath\n    ? `/api/images?path=${encodeURIComponent(qrisImagePath)}`\n    : null;\n\n  const downloadQris = () => {\n    if (!qrisUrl) return;\n    const a = document.createElement('a');\n    a.href = qrisUrl;\n    a.download = `QRIS-${orderToken}.png`;\n    a.click();\n  };\n\n  const handleConfirmPaid = async () => {\n    setConfirming(true);\n    const res = await markQrisPaidAction(orderToken);\n    setConfirming(false);\n    if (!res.ok) {\n      push(res.error ?? 'Gagal', 'error');\n      return;\n    }\n    setDone(true);\n    push('Terima kasih! Pembayaran menunggu verifikasi admin.', 'success');\n    onPaid?.();\n  };\n\n  const handleProof = async (file: File) => {\n    setUploading(true);\n    const fd = new FormData();\n    fd.append('file', file);\n    const res = await submitPaymentProofAction(orderToken, fd);\n    setUploading(false);\n    if (!res.ok) {\n      push(res.error ?? 'Gagal', 'error');\n      return;\n    }\n    setDone(true);\n    push('Bukti transfer terkirim — menunggu verifikasi admin.', 'success');\n    onPaid?.();\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <Modal open={open} onClose={onClose} title={done ? 'Pembayaran Diterima' : 'Bayar via QRIS'} size=\"sm\">\n      {done ? (\n        <div className=\"space-y-4 text-center\">\n          <CheckCircle2 className=\"mx-auto h-14 w-14 text-success\" aria-hidden />\n          <p className=\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\">\n            Kami sudah mencatat pembayaranmu untuk pesanan{' '}\n            <span className=\"font-mono font-bold text-foreground\">{orderToken}</span>. Admin akan\n            memverifikasi — pantau statusnya di halaman pesanan atau WhatsApp.\n          </p>\n          <Button className=\"w-full\" onClick={onClose}>\n            Lihat Status Pesanan\n          </Button>\n        </div>\n      ) : (\n        <div className=\"space-y-4\">\n          {/* QR */}\n          <div className=\"rounded-2xl border border-border bg-white p-3\">\n            {qrisUrl ? (\n              /* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */\n              <img src={qrisUrl} alt=\"QRIS\" className=\"mx-auto aspect-square w-full max-w-56 rounded-lg object-contain\" />\n            ) : (\n              <div className=\"mx-auto flex aspect-square w-full max-w-56 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border-2 border-dashed border-border text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground\">\n                QR belum dipasang admin — hubungi kami untuk bayar manual.\n              </div>\n            )}\n          </div>\n\n          <div className=\"space-y-1 text-center text-sm\">\n            <p className=\"text-muted-foreground\">\n              Total bayar:{' '}\n              <span className=\"text-base font-extrabold text-primary\">{formatRupiah(total)}</span>\n            </p>\n            {qrisReceiverName && (\n              <p className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\">Penerima: {qrisReceiverName}</p>\n            )}\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\">\n              Pesanan <span className=\"font-mono font-bold\">{orderToken}</span>\n            </p>\n          </div>\n\n          {/* Download QR */}\n          {qrisUrl && (\n            <Button variant=\"outline\" className=\"w-full\" onClick={downloadQris}>\n              <Download className=\"h-4 w-4\" /> Download QR\n            </Button>\n          )}\n\n          {/* Konfirmasi + bukti TF */}\n          <div className=\"space-y-2 border-t border-border pt-3\">\n            <p className=\"text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground\">\n              Sudah transfer? Konfirmasi di bawah — verifikasi manual oleh admin.\n            </p>\n            <Button className=\"w-full\" loading={confirming} onClick={handleConfirmPaid}>\n              <CheckCircle2 className=\"h-4 w-4\" /> Sudah Bayar\n            </Button>\n\n            <label className=\"block\">\n              <input\n                type=\"file\"\n                accept=\"image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp\"\n                className=\"hidden\"\n                onChange={(e) => {\n                  const f = e.target.files?.[0];\n                  if (f) handleProof(f);\n                  e.target.value = '';\n                }}\n              />\n              <span className=\"flex h-10 w-full cursor-pointer items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-border text-sm font-semibold transition-colors hover:bg-muted\">\n                <Upload className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n                {uploading ? 'Mengunggah...' : 'Kirim Bukti Transfer (opsional)'}\n              </span>\n            </label>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </Modal>\n  );\n}\n"
}
```

Typecheck bersih. Lanjut README + LICENSE + .env.example, lalu test:

**🛠 Write File**

````json
{
  "path": "README.md",
  "instructions": "Tulis ulang README: Supabase sebagai database utama, open source MIT, dokumentasi semua fitur baru (popup QRIS, prefill, WA berpetunjuk, pengumuman).",
  "content": "# Kedai Rasa — UMKM Food Ordering 🍜\n\n<p align=\"center\">\n  <img src=\"src/logo/logo.png\" alt=\"Kedai Rasa\" width=\"96\" />\n</p>\n\nAplikasi pemesanan makanan UMKM **realtime** — simple untuk pelanggan (tanpa login!), powerful untuk pemilik usaha.\n\n**Stack:** Next.js 16 (App Router) · TypeScript · Tailwind CSS v4 · **Supabase (PostgreSQL)** · Drizzle ORM · Vitest\n\n> ⭐ Proyek ini **open source** (MIT). Kontribusi dipersilakan — lihat bagian [Kontribusi](#-kontribusi).\n\n---\n\n## ✨ Fitur\n\n### 👤 Customer — tanpa daftar akun\n- **Token session** `UMKM-XXXXX-XXXXX` — masuk tanpa login; token disimpan sebagai **hash SHA-256** di DB, cookie httpOnly\n- **Data pemesan tersimpan** — nama & No. HP dari pesanan pertama otomatis jadi default di pesanan berikutnya (tetap bisa diedit)\n- **Menu** — filter kategori, pencarian, status stok realtime (Masih Banyak / Sedikit Lagi / Habis)\n- **Keranjang** — localStorage, deteksi produk habis realtime\n- **Checkout** — QRIS / COD, kode promo, catatan\n- **Popup QRIS saat checkout** — QR langsung tampil setelah order dibuat: **download QR**, tombol **“Sudah Bayar”**, dan **upload bukti transfer**\n- **Tracking realtime** — timeline status pesanan, update tanpa refresh (SSE)\n- **Pengaturan** — tema light/dark, info toko, peta, kontak\n\n### 🛠️ Admin — login email + password\n- **Dashboard** — pendapatan & pesanan hari ini, grafik 7 hari, produk terlaris, order baru realtime\n- **Pesanan** — filter status, transisi tervalidasi, verifikasi pembayaran + lihat **bukti transfer** customer\n- **Produk & Kategori** — CRUD + upload gambar\n- **Promo** — persentase / nominal, minimum belanja, maks diskon, periode\n- **Laporan** — harian / mingguan / bulanan / tahunan + analitik\n- **Toko** — info, logo, status buka/tutup (otomatis dari jam operasional atau paksa), jam buka 7 hari, **upload foto QRIS**, COD on/off\n- **Pengumuman** — 1 tombol: caption buka/tutup otomatis dari data terkini, payload WhatsApp broadcast, dan link **poster publik `/poster`** (logo + status + jam hari ini + 3 produk yang pasti tersedia — tidak ada lagi poster basi)\n\n### 🔔 Notifikasi WhatsApp (berpetunjuk)\nPesan WA tidak hanya memberi tahu status, tapi juga **mengarahkan customer**:\n- Simpan token selama pesanan berjalan (token ditulis di pesan)\n- Pantau status di halaman pesanan — tidak perlu chat admin dulu\n- Info refund saat pesanan ditolak, ucapan terima kasih saat selesai\n\n### 🔒 Keamanan\n- Semua harga, diskon, promo **dihitung ulang di server** — input browser tidak dipercaya\n- Token customer di-hash; raw token hanya di cookie httpOnly\n- Order wajib milik session pemanggil (customer tidak bisa lihat pesanan orang lain)\n- Transisi status order tervalidasi (tidak bisa lompat status)\n- Upload: whitelist MIME + maks 2MB; serving gambar anti path-traversal\n- Semua server action admin berawal `requireAdmin()`\n\n---\n\n## 🚀 Mulai Cepat (Supabase)\n\n### 0. Prasyarat\n- Node.js 20+\n- Akun **Supabase** gratis → [supabase.com](https://supabase.com)\n\n### 1. Setup proyek Supabase\n1. Buat proyek baru → tunggu provisioning selesai\n2. **SQL Editor** → jalankan seluruh file `supabase/migrations/001_initial.sql`, lalu `002_seed.sql`\n3. **Project Settings → Database → Connection string → Pooler** (port **6543**, mode Transaction) → salin URI\n\n### 2. Install & konfigurasi\n```bash\ngit clone https://github.com/username/kedai-rasa.git\ncd kedai-rasa\nnpm install\ncp .env.example .env.local\n```\n\nIsi `.env.local`:\n```env\n# Supabase pooler (port 6543) — WAJIB lewat pooler, bukan direct 5432\nDATABASE_URL=postgresql://postgres.xxxx:PASSWORD@aws-0-region.pooler.supabase.com:6543/postgres\n\nAUTH_SECRET=hasil-generate-di-bawah\nADMIN_EMAIL=admin@tokoku.id\nADMIN_PASSWORD=password-kuat-minimal-8\nADMIN_NAME=Admin Toko\n\nWHATSAPP_PROVIDER=mock        # produksi: fonnte + WHATSAPP_TOKEN\n```\n\nGenerate `AUTH_SECRET` (wajib, min 32 karakter):\n```bash\nnode -e \"console.log(require('crypto').randomBytes(48).toString('base64url'))\"\n```\n\n> 💡 **Kenapa port 6543?** Koneksi Drizzle/postgres.js ke Supabase **wajib** `prepare: false` (sudah diset di `src/db/index.ts`) dan lewat **pooler transaction mode**, kalau tidak akan muncul error intermiten `prepared statement does not exist` pada query paralel.\n\n### 3. Jalankan\n```bash\nnpm run dev\n```\n\n| Sisi | URL | Kredensial |\n|---|---|---|\n| Customer | http://localhost:3000 | tanpa login — klik “Buat Token Baru” |\n| Admin | http://localhost:3000/admin/login | dari `ADMIN_EMAIL` / `ADMIN_PASSWORD` |\n\n### 4. Test\n```bash\nnpm test   # unit test: token, promo, status toko, pengumuman, broadcast\n```\n\n---\n\n## 🏗️ Arsitektur\n\n```\nsrc/\n├── app/\n│   ├── page.tsx                 # Token gate (customer)\n│   ├── poster/                  # Poster publik dinamis (share medsos)\n│   ├── actions/                 # Server action customer (session, checkout, QRIS)\n│   ├── (customer)/              # Route group terproteksi: menu, cart, checkout, orders, settings\n│   ├── admin/\n│   │   ├── actions.ts           # Server action admin (requireAdmin di awal)\n│   │   └── (dashboard)/         # Dashboard, orders, products, promos, reports, store\n│   └── api/\n│       ├── realtime/            # SSE endpoint (realtime lokal)\n│       └── images/              # Serving gambar upload (anti path-traversal)\n├── components/                  # ui/ (design system) + domain/ (bisnis)\n├── services/                    # Business logic: order, catalog, promo, store,\n│                                # report, whatsapp, admin-auth, customer-session, realtime\n├── lib/                         # token, storage, validations (zod)\n├── db/                          # Drizzle schema + koneksi (prepare:false utk pooler)\n├── hooks/                       # useCart, useTheme, useRealtime\n└── utils/                       # format, token, promo, status toko, teks WA/pengumuman\n```\n\n### Alur order (server-side penuh)\n```\nKeranjang (client)\n  → createOrderAction → validasi session + zod\n  → cek status toko (buka/tutup)\n  → ambil harga dari DB (bukan dari browser)\n  → validasi stok per item + hitung promo\n  → TRANSACTION: orders + order_items + payments + histories + promo_usages\n  → simpan nama & No. HP ke customers (prefill pesanan berikutnya)\n  → realtime: admin + customer\n  → WhatsApp notification (best-effort)\n  → (QRIS) popup bayar: download QR / Sudah Bayar / upload bukti TF\n```\n\n### Realtime\nSSE via `/api/realtime` dengan topik:\n- `store` — status toko berubah\n- `products` — ketersediaan produk berubah\n- `order:{session_id}` — update pesanan milik satu customer saja\n- `admin` — order baru, bukti bayar baru (khusus admin login)\n\n---\n\n## 🤝 Kontribusi\n\n1. Fork → buat branch (`git checkout -b fitur-keren`)\n2. `npm test` dan `npx tsc --noEmit` harus hijau sebelum commit\n3. Commit deskriptif, lalu Pull Request\n4. Untuk perubahan besar, buka Issue dulu untuk diskusi\n\n## 📄 Lisensi\n\nDilisensikan di bawah [MIT License](LICENSE) — bebas dipakai, dimodifikasi, dan didistribusikan. Cocok untuk UMKM mana pun yang ingin Go-digital. 🇮🇩\n\n---\n\n*Dibuat dengan Next.js 16, Tailwind CSS v4, Drizzle ORM, Supabase, dan banyak kopi.* ☕\n"
}
````

```

- path: src/app/api/images/route.ts
  content: |
      import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
      import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
      import { join, normalize, extname } from 'node:path';
      
      const MIME: Record<string, string> = {
        '.jpg': 'image/jpeg',
        '.jpeg': 'image/jpeg',
        '.png': 'image/png',
        '.webp': 'image/webp',
      };
      
      /**
       * Serve gambar dari public/uploads.
       * Path divalidasi ketat: hanya [folder]/[filename] di dalam uploads.
       */
      export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
        const raw = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get('path') ?? '';
        const normalized = normalize(raw).replace(/^(\.\.(\/|\\|$))+/, '');
      
        // Tolak path traversal
        if (
          !normalized ||
          normalized.startsWith('.') ||
          normalized.includes('..') ||
          normalized.startsWith('/')
        ) {
          return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Path tidak valid' }, { status: 400 });
        }
      
        const ext = extname(normalized).toLowerCase();
        if (!MIME[ext]) {
          return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Format tidak didukung' }, { status: 400 });
        }
      
        const filePath = join(process.cwd(), 'public', 'uploads', normalized);
      
        try {
          const data = await readFile(filePath);
          return new NextResponse(new Uint8Array(data), {
            headers: {
              'Content-Type': MIME[ext],
              'Cache-Control': 'public, max-age=31536000, immutable',
            },
          });
        } catch {
          return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Gambar tidak ditemukan' }, { status: 404 });
        }
      }
      
  referencedBy: {}
```

> _Reasoning_
> Audit quick pass results (looking at what I've seen across this session):
> 
> 1. **`/api/images` path traversal** — validated: rejects `..`, leading `.`, leading `/`, MIME whitelist. The normalize + checks look solid. One subtle thing: `normalize(raw)` on Windows-style backslashes... `normalized.includes('..')` catches most. Backslash isn't a separator on posix; `join` would treat `a\..\b` as one filename — safe on Linux. OK.
> 
> 2. **`uploadImageAction`** admin-only via requireAdmin ✓. My new customer proof upload validates ownership + MIME + size ✓.
> 
> 3. **Customer proof files stored in `public/uploads/proofs`** — hmm, PUBLIC folder! Files in `public/uploads` are served statically by Next.js without auth (that's how /api/images works too — it serves from public/uploads without auth either!). Wait — `/api/images` serves any upload without authentication. Product images/store logo/QRIS are public by design. But **payment proofs** are sensitive (contains customer's bank/transfer info) — being publicly accessible at `/uploads/proofs/xxx.jpg` (static) or via `/api/images?path=proofs/xxx.jpg` (no auth) is a **privacy hole**. Also predictability: UUID filenames make guessing hard. But the admin views the proof... does the admin order page even show proof? Not yet — the admin order detail page doesn't display proofPath. I should: (a) move proofs OUT of public? That changes storage serving. Simpler mitigation: keep in uploads but note it; better: since filename is UUID (unguessable), risk is low-ish, but static serving from /public means anyone with the URL can access. There's no URL leak except admin/customer pages.
> 
>    Pragmatic fix within scope: display the proof in admin order detail (needed feature anyway) and add a note. Moving proofs to a non-public dir + auth'd serving would be the proper fix — that's a bigger change. Given "audit ringkas", I'll flag it in the report as a known gap with recommendation, and display proof in admin detail so the feature is complete.
> 
>    Actually wait — static files in `public/uploads` are served by Next static handler. But files written at RUNTIME to `public/uploads` — in dev, Next serves them; in production standalone builds, `public` is copied at build time, so runtime-written files may not be served statically at all (that's why `/api/images` route exists!). So static access isn't actually the vector; `/api/images` is, and it's unauthenticated. Risk: someone who knows/guesses the proof path (UUID) can view. Low likelihood, but the *admin* also can't view proofs without me adding UI. Let me add proof display in admin order detail — that completes the loop — and flag the unauthenticated image serving as audit finding.
> 
> 4. **`createOrder`**: prices from DB ✓, stock validated ✓, promo server-side ✓. Missing: quantity > 0 validated by zod ✓; no per-product qty cap besides 99 ✓. Duplicate productId in items array → lineItems would double-count and `productRows.length !== productIds.length` check uses lengths — duplicates pass (length equal), creating two lines for same product. Harmless-ish (customer pays correctly per line). Not a bug per se.
> 
> 5. **Session revocation on completed** ✓ with active-order check.
> 
> 6. **`getTodayOrders`** uses server-local midnight (not store TZ) — minor inconsistency with report.ts which is TZ-aware. Flag as minor.
> 
> 7. **Rate limiting** — only admin login has rateLimitKey. Customer token creation (`createSessionAction`) is unlimited → a malicious actor could spam-create sessions/customers rows. Flag as finding. Also `restoreSessionAction` brute force: token space huge (32^10), format-validated; okay-ish.
> 
> 8. **DB index.ts prepare:false** ✓ fixed.
> 
> 9. **admin auth**: bcrypt + session? I haven't read admin-auth.ts fully, but loginAction has rate limit key, requireAdmin used everywhere I saw. Fine for ringkas.
> 
> 10. **Realtime JWT** — AUTH_SECRET-based, min 32 chars enforced ✓.
> 
> 11. **Checkout popup edge**: If store has no QR image, popup shows placeholder — good.
> 
> 12. `getCustomerProfileAction` returns ok:false when no session — fine.
> 
> One more important check: `submitPaymentProofAction` — customer can re-upload repeatedly (overwrites proofPath each time, status stays waiting_verification). Acceptable.
> 
> Also — admin order detail should show the proof image + receiver name for verification. Let me add a small section in admin orders/[id]/page.tsx: if paymentMethod qris && payment.proofPath → show image. Order type has payment.proofPath already (hydrated in getOrderByTokenForSession, but getOrderByIdForAdmin does NOT include payment!). It only spreads histories. So admin page lacks payment info. Quick fix: include payment in getOrderByIdForAdmin like the customer one. Then show in UI.
> 
> Let me implement:
> 1. order.ts — add payment hydration in getOrderByIdForAdmin.
> 2. Admin order detail — show QRIS receiver + proof image if exists.
> 3. Update todos, run typecheck+tests, then final summary with feature explanation + audit findings.
> 
> Also should update OrderWithDetails type? It already has `payment?: {...} | null` I believe (customer path sets it). Check types: line 100 `proofPath: string | null;` inside payment type — yes OrderWithDetails has payment optional.

```
- path: src/services/order.ts
  content: |
        };
      }
      
      /** Detail order untuk admin (tanpa batasan session). */
      export async function getOrderByIdForAdmin(orderId: string): Promise<OrderWithDetails | null> {
        const admin = await getAdminSession();
        if (!admin) return null;
      
        const [row] = await db.select().from(orders).where(eq(orders.id, orderId)).limit(1);
        if (!row) return null;
      
        const [hydrated] = await hydrateOrders([row]);
        const histories = await db
          .select()
          .from(orderStatusHistories)
          .where(eq(orderStatusHistories.orderId, row.id))
          .orderBy(asc(orderStatusHistories.createdAt));
      
        // Payment (admin perlu lihat bukti transfer & status verifikasi)
        const [payment] = await db.select().from(payments).where(eq(payments.orderId, row.id)).limit(1);
      
        return {
          ...hydrated,
          histories: histories.map((h) => ({
            id: h.id,
            status: h.status,
            note: h.note,
            changedBy: h.changedBy,
            createdAt: h.createdAt.toISOString(),
          })),
          payment: payment
            ? {
                method: payment.method,
                status: payment.status,
                reference: payment.reference,
                proofPath: payment.proofPath,
                verifiedAt: payment.verifiedAt ? payment.verifiedAt.toISOString() : null,
              }
            : null,
        };
```

## Assistant

Set up your own provider key (BYOK). BYOK runs never spend Freebucks.

BYOK uses your provider account directly. Keys are read from an environment variable and never stored in Freebuff.
Use the environment-variable NAME only, for example OPENROUTER_API_KEY. Never paste an API key into this command.
Usage:
`/byok list`
`/byok add <name> <openrouter|openai-compatible> <model> <ENV_VAR> [base-url] [--context-window=N] [--max-output-tokens=N]`
`/byok update <name> <model> [base-url] [--context-window=N] [--max-output-tokens=N]`
`/byok validate <name>`
`/byok select <name>`
`/byok remove <name>`
`/byok off`

Add a connection, then `/byok select <name>` to start. `/byok off` returns to Freebuff.
